<a id="surface-fluxscale"></a>

## Fluxscale (<code>fluxscale</code>)

- Kind: `task`
- Contract version: `7`
- Category: Calibration
- Provider family: `calibration`
- Summary: Scale gain solutions using reference calibrator fields.

| Parameter | Concept ID / revision | Type / unit | Default / required | Group | Summary |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| <code>vis</code> | <code>data.input.vis@r2</code> | <code>path (measurement_set)</code> | <code>""</code>; required | Input | One input MeasurementSet path.<br><em>Surface:</em> Canonical vis maps to provider field measurement_set. CASA's fluxscale requires the MeasurementSet the gain table was solved from. |
| <code>caltable</code> | <code>scaling.caltable@r1</code> | <code>path (calibration_table)</code> | <code>""</code>; required | Fluxscale | Gain table fluxscale reads, solved for the reference and transfer fields. |
| <code>fluxtable</code> | <code>scaling.fluxtable@r1</code> | <code>path (calibration_table)</code> | <code>""</code>; required | Fluxscale | Flux-scaled gain table fluxscale writes. |
| <code>reference</code> | <code>scaling.reference@r2</code> | <code>array&lt;string&gt;</code> | <code>[]</code>; required | Fluxscale | Fields whose flux densities are known. |
| <code>transfer</code> | <code>scaling.transfer@r2</code> | <code>array&lt;string&gt;</code> | <code>[]</code>; optional | Fluxscale | Fields whose flux densities are derived; empty for all the others. |
| <code>listfile</code> | <code>scaling.listfile@r1</code> | <code>string</code> | <code>""</code>; optional | Fluxscale | Text file that receives the derived flux densities; empty for none. |
| <code>append</code> | <code>scaling.append@r1</code> | <code>bool</code> | <code>false</code>; optional | Fluxscale | Append fluxscale's solutions to an existing flux table. |
| <code>refspwmap</code> | <code>scaling.refspwmap@r2</code> | <code>array&lt;integer&gt;</code> | <code>[-1]</code>; optional | Fluxscale | Reference window for each spectral window; [-1] for none. |
| <code>gainthreshold</code> | <code>scaling.gainthreshold@r2</code> | <code>float</code> | <code>-1.0</code>; optional | Fluxscale | Fraction from the median outside which gain amplitudes are excluded; -1 for none. |
| <code>antenna</code> | <code>ms.selection.antenna@r2</code> | <code>string</code> | <code>""</code>; optional | Selection | CASA antenna (baseline) selection, exact text |
| <code>timerange</code> | <code>ms.selection.timerange@r2</code> | <code>string</code> | <code>""</code>; optional | Selection | CASA time selection, exact text |
| <code>scan</code> | <code>ms.selection.scan@r2</code> | <code>string</code> | <code>""</code>; optional | Selection | Syntax-preserving numeric scan selection |
| <code>incremental</code> | <code>parameter.incremental@r1</code> | <code>bool</code> | <code>false</code>; optional | Fluxscale | Write incremental correction factors instead of absolute transfer gains |
| <code>fitorder</code> | <code>scaling.fitorder@r2</code> | <code>integer</code> | <code>1</code>; optional | Fluxscale | Polynomial order of the spectral-index fit across spectral windows. |
| <code>display</code> | <code>scaling.display@r1</code> | <code>bool</code> | <code>false</code>; optional | Fluxscale | Plot the fitted flux-density spectra. |
| <code>output</code> | <code>calibration.report.output@r1</code> | <code>path (any)</code> | <code>""</code>; optional | Output | Optional destination for a calibration task report. |
| <code>overwrite</code> | <code>output.overwrite@r1</code> | <code>bool</code> | <code>false</code>; optional | Output | Replace an existing output file |
| <code>format</code> | <code>parameter.format@r1</code> | <code>choice (2 values)</code> | <code>"text"</code>; optional | Output | Execution report format |

| <code>antenna</code> | <code>ms.selection.antenna@r4</code> | <code>string</code> | <code>""</code>; optional | Data selection | Applycal antenna selector text. |
| <code>scan</code> | <code>ms.selection.scan@r4</code> | <code>string</code> | <code>""</code>; optional | Data selection | Applycal scan selector text. |

| <code>intent</code> | <code>ms.selection.intent@r3</code> | <code>string</code> | <code>""</code>; optional | Data selection | Intent selector. |
| <code>model</code> | <code>ft.model@r2</code> | <code>variant</code> | <code>""</code>; optional | Model | A model image name or a list of model image names. |
| <code>nterms</code> | <code>parameter.nterms@r1</code> | <code>integer</code> | <code>1</code>; optional | Model | Number of Taylor terms. |

<a id="surface-gencal"></a>

## Gencal (<code>gencal</code>)

- Kind: `task`
- Contract version: `6`
- Category: Calibration
- Provider family: `calibration`
- Summary: Generate a calibration table such as antpos, gceff, or opac.

| Parameter | Concept ID / revision | Type / unit | Default / required | Group | Summary |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| <code>vis</code> | <code>data.input.vis@r1</code> | <code>array&lt;path (measurement_set)&gt;</code> | <code>""</code>; required | Input | Input MeasurementSet path or paths.<br><em>Surface:</em> Canonical vis maps to provider field measurement_set. This surface uses the MeasurementSet as calibration workflow input. |
| <code>caltable</code> | <code>calibration.caltable@r1</code> | <code>path (calibration_table)</code> | <code>""</code>; required | Solve | Calibration table the task writes. |
| <code>caltype</code> | <code>specifycal.caltype@r1</code> | <code>choice (20 values)</code> | <code>""</code>; required | Gencal | Type of calibration gencal specifies. |
| <code>infile</code> | <code>specifycal.infile@r1</code> | <code>string</code> | <code>""</code>; optional | Gencal | File some calibration types read: weather, EOP or a Jy/K table. |
| <code>endpoint</code> | <code>specifycal.endpoint@r1</code> | <code>string</code> | <code>"asdm"</code>; optional | Gencal | ALMA Jy/K database query endpoint. |
| <code>timeout</code> | <code>specifycal.timeout@r1</code> | <code>integer</code> | <code>180</code>; optional | Gencal | Seconds the antenna-position web service has to answer. |
| <code>retry</code> | <code>specifycal.retry@r1</code> | <code>integer</code> | <code>3</code>; optional | Gencal | Number of attempts to contact the ALMA Jy/K database. |
| <code>retry_wait_time</code> | <code>specifycal.retry_wait_time@r1</code> | <code>integer</code> | <code>5</code>; optional | Gencal | Seconds between requests to the antenna-position web service. |
| <code>ant_pos_time_limit</code> | <code>specifycal.ant_pos_time_limit@r1</code> | <code>integer</code> | <code>0</code>; optional | Gencal | Days before or after the observation an antenna-position correction may come from. |
| <code>spw</code> | <code>ms.selection.spw@r1</code> | <code>string</code> | <code>""</code>; optional | Selection | Comma-separated SPECTRAL_WINDOW_ID integers |
| <code>antenna</code> | <code>ms.selection.antenna@r1</code> | <code>string</code> | <code>""</code>; optional | Selection | Comma-separated antenna ids matched against ANTENNA1/ANTENNA2 |
| <code>pol</code> | <code>specifycal.pol@r1</code> | <code>string</code> | <code>""</code>; optional | Gencal | Polarizations the calibration applies to; empty for all. |
| <code>parameter</code> | <code>specifycal.parameter@r1</code> | <code>array&lt;float&gt;</code> | <code>[]</code>; optional | Gencal | Values of the calibration, per antenna, window and polarization as caltype defines. |
| <code>uniform</code> | <code>specifycal.uniform@r1</code> | <code>bool</code> | <code>true</code>; optional | Gencal | Take one Jy/K factor for every antenna. |
| <code>output</code> | <code>calibration.report.output@r1</code> | <code>path (any)</code> | <code>""</code>; optional | Output | Optional destination for a calibration task report. |
| <code>overwrite</code> | <code>output.overwrite@r1</code> | <code>bool</code> | <code>false</code>; optional | Output | Replace an existing output file |
| <code>format</code> | <code>parameter.format@r1</code> | <code>choice (2 values)</code> | <code>"text"</code>; optional | Output | Execution report format |
| <code>gaincurve_table</code> | <code>parameter.gaincurve_table@r1</code> | <code>path (calibration_table)</code> | <code>""</code>; optional | Gencal | Optional gaincurve input table for gceff. |

| <code>veldef</code> | <code>plotms.veldef@r1</code> | <code>choice (3 values)</code> | <code>"RADIO"</code>; optional | Transforms | The definition in which to render velocity |
| <code>phasecenter</code> | <code>plotms.phasecenter@r1</code> | <code>string</code> | <code>""</code>; optional | Transforms | The direction coordinates of the desired center |
| <code>extendflag</code> | <code>plotms.extendflag@r1</code> | <code>bool</code> | <code>false</code>; optional | Flagging | Extend flagging to other data points not plotted |
| <code>extcorr</code> | <code>plotms.extcorr@r1</code> | <code>bool</code> | <code>false</code>; optional | Flagging | Extend flags based on correlation |
| <code>extchannel</code> | <code>plotms.extchannel@r1</code> | <code>bool</code> | <code>false</code>; optional | Flagging | Extend flags based on channel |
| <code>iteraxis</code> | <code>plotms.iteraxis@r1</code> | <code>choice (21 values)</code> | <code>""</code>; optional | Presentation | The axis over which to iterate |
| <code>xselfscale</code> | <code>parameter.xselfscale@r1</code> | <code>bool</code> | <code>false</code>; optional | Presentation | Use per-panel X bounds on iterated plots |
| <code>yselfscale</code> | <code>parameter.yselfscale@r1</code> | <code>bool</code> | <code>false</code>; optional | Presentation | Use per-panel Y bounds on iterated plots |
| <code>xsharedaxis</code> | <code>parameter.xsharedaxis@r1</code> | <code>bool</code> | <code>false</code>; optional | Presentation | Force a shared X axis across iterated panels |
| <code>ysharedaxis</code> | <code>parameter.ysharedaxis@r1</code> | <code>bool</code> | <code>false</code>; optional | Presentation | Force a shared Y axis across iterated panels |
| <code>customsymbol</code> | <code>plotms.customsymbol@r1</code> | <code>variant</code> | <code>[]</code>; optional | Presentation | Enable custom symbol(s) for unflagged points |
| <code>symbolshape</code> | <code>plotms.symbolshape@r1</code> | <code>variant</code> | <code>"autoscaling"</code>; optional | Presentation | Shape of plotted unflagged symbols |
| <code>symbolsize</code> | <code>plotms.symbolsize@r1</code> | <code>variant</code> | <code>2</code>; optional | Presentation | Size of plotted unflagged symbols |
| <code>symbolcolor</code> | <code>plotms.symbolcolor@r1</code> | <code>variant</code> | <code>"0000ff"</code>; optional | Presentation | Color (name or hex code) of plotted unflagged symbols |
| <code>symbolfill</code> | <code>plotms.symbolfill@r1</code> | <code>variant</code> | <code>"fill"</code>; optional | Presentation | Fill type of plotted unflagged symbols |
| <code>symboloutline</code> | <code>plotms.symboloutline@r1</code> | <code>variant</code> | <code>false</code>; optional | Presentation | Outline plotted unflagged symbols |
| <code>coloraxis</code> | <code>plotms.coloraxis@r1</code> | <code>choice (32 values)</code> | <code>""</code>; optional | Presentation | Selects data axis for colorizing |
| <code>customflaggedsymbol</code> | <code>plotms.customflaggedsymbol@r1</code> | <code>variant</code> | <code>false</code>; optional | Presentation | Enable custom symbol(s) for flagged points |
| <code>flaggedsymbolshape</code> | <code>plotms.flaggedsymbolshape@r1</code> | <code>variant</code> | <code>"circle"</code>; optional | Presentation | Shape of plotted flagged symbols |
| <code>flaggedsymbolsize</code> | <code>plotms.flaggedsymbolsize@r1</code> | <code>variant</code> | <code>2</code>; optional | Presentation | Size of plotted flagged symbols |
| <code>flaggedsymbolcolor</code> | <code>plotms.flaggedsymbolcolor@r1</code> | <code>variant</code> | <code>"ff0000"</code>; optional | Presentation | Color (name or hex code) of plotted flagged symbols |
| <code>flaggedsymbolfill</code> | <code>plotms.flaggedsymbolfill@r1</code> | <code>variant</code> | <code>"fill"</code>; optional | Presentation | Fill type of plotted flagged symbols |
| <code>flaggedsymboloutline</code> | <code>plotms.flaggedsymboloutline@r1</code> | <code>variant</code> | <code>false</code>; optional | Presentation | Outline plotted flagged symbols |
| <code>xconnector</code> | <code>plotms.xconnector@r1</code> | <code>choice (4 values)</code> | <code>""</code>; optional | Presentation | Set connector for data points (blank="none"; "line","step") |
| <code>timeconnector</code> | <code>plotms.timeconnector@r1</code> | <code>bool</code> | <code>false</code>; optional | Presentation | Connect points by time rather than x-axis |
| <code>plotrange</code> | <code>plotbandpass.plotrange@r1</code> | <code>array&lt;float&gt;</code> | <code>[]</code>; optional | Axes | The axes limits to use [x0,x1,y0,y1]. |
| <code>title</code> | <code>plotms.title@r1</code> | <code>string</code> | <code>""</code>; optional | Presentation | Title at top of plot |
| <code>titlefont</code> | <code>plotms.titlefont@r1</code> | <code>integer</code> | <code>0</code>; optional | Presentation | Font size for plot title |
| <code>xlabel</code> | <code>plotms.xlabel@r1</code> | <code>string</code> | <code>""</code>; optional | Presentation | Text for horizontal x-axis. Blank for default. |
| <code>xaxisfont</code> | <code>plotms.xaxisfont@r1</code> | <code>integer</code> | <code>0</code>; optional | Presentation | Font size for x-axis label |
| <code>ylabel</code> | <code>plotms.ylabel@r1</code> | <code>string</code> | <code>""</code>; optional | Presentation | Text for vertical y-axis. Blank for default. |
| <code>yaxisfont</code> | <code>plotms.yaxisfont@r1</code> | <code>integer</code> | <code>0</code>; optional | Presentation | Font size for y-axis label |
| <code>showmajorgrid</code> | <code>parameter.showmajorgrid@r1</code> | <code>bool</code> | <code>false</code>; optional | Presentation | Show major grid lines |
| <code>majorwidth</code> | <code>plotms.majorwidth@r1</code> | <code>integer</code> | <code>1</code>; optional | Presentation | Line width in pixels of major grid lines |
| <code>majorstyle</code> | <code>plotms.majorstyle@r1</code> | <code>choice (5 values)</code> | <code>""</code>; optional | Presentation | Major grid line style |
| <code>majorcolor</code> | <code>plotms.majorcolor@r1</code> | <code>string</code> | <code>"B0B0B0"</code>; optional | Presentation | Color (name or hex code) of major grid lines |
| <code>showminorgrid</code> | <code>parameter.showminorgrid@r1</code> | <code>bool</code> | <code>false</code>; optional | Presentation | Show minor grid lines |
| <code>minorwidth</code> | <code>plotms.minorwidth@r1</code> | <code>integer</code> | <code>1</code>; optional | Presentation | Line width in pixels of minor grid lines |
| <code>minorstyle</code> | <code>plotms.minorstyle@r1</code> | <code>choice (5 values)</code> | <code>""</code>; optional | Presentation | Minor grid line style |
| <code>minorcolor</code> | <code>plotms.minorcolor@r1</code> | <code>string</code> | <code>"D0D0D0"</code>; optional | Presentation | Color (name or hex code) of minor grid lines |
| <code>showlegend</code> | <code>parameter.showlegend@r1</code> | <code>bool</code> | <code>false</code>; optional | Presentation | Show a legend for grouped series |
| <code>legendposition</code> | <code>plotms.legendposition@r1</code> | <code>choice (9 values)</code> | <code>""</code>; optional | Presentation | Legend position, default upperRight. |
| <code>plotfile</code> | <code>plotms.plotfile@r1</code> | <code>path (file)</code> | <code>""</code>; optional | Export | Name of plot file to save automatically |
| <code>expformat</code> | <code>plotms.expformat@r1</code> | <code>choice (6 values)</code> | <code>""</code>; optional | Export | Export format type. If not provided, plotfile extension will be used to determine type. |
| <code>verbose</code> | <code>plotms.verbose@r1</code> | <code>bool</code> | <code>true</code>; optional | Export | Include metadata in text export |
| <code>exprange</code> | <code>plotms.exprange@r1</code> | <code>choice (3 values)</code> | <code>""</code>; optional | Export | Range of iteration plots to export, one plotfile per page. Multipage pdf exports are not supported. |
| <code>highres</code> | <code>plotms.highres@r1</code> | <code>bool</code> | <code>false</code>; optional | Export | Use high resolution |
| <code>dpi</code> | <code>plotms.dpi@r1</code> | <code>integer</code> | <code>-1</code>; optional | Export | DPI of exported plot |
| <code>width</code> | <code>plotms.width@r1</code> | <code>integer</code> | <code>-1</code>; optional | Export | Width in pixels of exported plot |
| <code>height</code> | <code>plotms.height@r1</code> | <code>integer</code> | <code>-1</code>; optional | Export | Height in pixels of exported plot |
| <code>overwrite</code> | <code>output.overwrite@r1</code> | <code>bool</code> | <code>false</code>; optional | Export | Replace an existing output file |
| <code>showgui</code> | <code>plotms.showgui@r1</code> | <code>bool</code> | <code>true</code>; optional | Export | Show GUI |
| <code>clearplots</code> | <code>plotms.clearplots@r1</code> | <code>bool</code> | <code>true</code>; optional | Presentation | Remove any existing plots so new ones can replace them. |
| <code>callib</code> | <code>plotms.callib@r1</code> | <code>array&lt;string&gt;</code> | <code>[]</code>; optional | Calibration | Calibration library string or filename for on-the-fly calibration. |
| <code>headeritems</code> | <code>plotms.headeritems@r1</code> | <code>string</code> | <code>""</code>; optional | Presentation | Comma-separated list of pre-defined page header items. |
| <code>showatm</code> | <code>plotbandpass.showatm@r1</code> | <code>bool</code> | <code>false</code>; optional | Atmosphere | Compute and overlay the atmospheric transmission curve |
| <code>showtsky</code> | <code>plotbandpass.showtsky@r1</code> | <code>bool</code> | <code>false</code>; optional | Atmosphere | Compute and overlay the sky temperature curve instead of transmission |
| <code>showimage</code> | <code>plotbandpass.showimage@r1</code> | <code>bool</code> | <code>false</code>; optional | Atmosphere | also show the atmospheric curve for the image sideband (in black) |
| <code>colorizeoverlay</code> | <code>plotms.colorizeoverlay@r1</code> | <code>bool</code> | <code>false</code>; optional | Atmosphere | Colorize the curve overlay using coloraxis |

<a id="surface-flagdata"></a>

## Flag Data (<code>flagdata</code>)

- Kind: `task`
- Contract version: `5`
- Category: Flagging
- Provider family: `flagdata`
- Summary: Run native CASA-style MeasurementSet flagging.

| Parameter | Concept ID / revision | Type / unit | Default / required | Group | Summary |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| <code>vis</code> | <code>data.input.vis@r1</code> | <code>array&lt;path (measurement_set)&gt;</code> | <code>""</code>; required | Input | Input MeasurementSet path or paths. |
| <code>mode</code> | <code>flagdata.mode@r2</code> | <code>choice (13 values)</code> | <code>"manual"</code>; optional | Flagging | Flagging mode. |
| <code>autocorr</code> | <code>flagdata.autocorr@r1</code> | <code>bool</code> | <code>false</code>; optional | Selection | Flag only the autocorrelations of the selection. |
| <code>inpfile</code> | <code>flagdata.inpfile@r1</code> | <code>variant</code> | <code>""</code>; optional | Input | File, files or list of flag commands to apply in list mode. |
| <code>reason</code> | <code>flagdata.reason@r1</code> | <code>variant</code> | <code>"any"</code>; optional | Input | Select list-mode flag commands by their reason. |
| <code>tbuff</code> | <code>flagdata.tbuff@r1</code> | <code>variant</code> | <code>0.0</code>; optional | Input | Time buffer in seconds that pads each flag command's time range. |
| <code>spw</code> | <code>ms.selection.spw@r6</code> | <code>variant</code> | <code>""</code>; optional | Selection | CASA flagdata spw selector. |
| <code>field</code> | <code>ms.selection.field@r8</code> | <code>variant</code> | <code>""</code>; optional | Selection | CASA flagdata field selector. |
| <code>antenna</code> | <code>ms.selection.antenna@r7</code> | <code>variant</code> | <code>""</code>; optional | Selection | CASA flagdata antenna selector. |
| <code>uvrange</code> | <code>ms.selection.uvrange@r4</code> | <code>variant</code> | <code>""</code>; optional | Selection | Accepts a CASA variant and converts it to selector text during MeasurementSet selection. |
| <code>timerange</code> | <code>ms.selection.timerange@r6</code> | <code>variant</code> | <code>""</code>; optional | Selection | CASA flagdata timerange selector. |
| <code>correlation</code> | <code>ms.selection.correlation@r3</code> | <code>variant</code> | <code>""</code>; optional | Selection | CASA flagging agent correlation selection.<br><em>Surface:</em> Selects stored correlation planes for manual, unflag, quack and summary. Clip and unaveraged TFCrop support ABS, REAL, IMAG, ARG and NORM on receptor labels, derived Stokes and WVR. Their defaults skip stored-I buffers. Summary counts stored flags for an operator expression. Automatic averaging and remaining mode combinations are tracked by #1616. |
| <code>scan</code> | <code>ms.selection.scan@r6</code> | <code>variant</code> | <code>""</code>; optional | Selection | CASA flagdata scan selector. |
| <code>intent</code> | <code>ms.selection.intent@r4</code> | <code>variant</code> | <code>""</code>; optional | Selection | CASA flagdata intent selector. |
| <code>array</code> | <code>ms.selection.array@r2</code> | <code>variant</code> | <code>""</code>; optional | Selection | CASA subarray selection by ARRAY_ID. |
| <code>observation</code> | <code>ms.selection.observation@r4</code> | <code>variant</code> | <code>""</code>; optional | Selection | Accepts selector text or an observation ID. Preserve the input type for HISTORY. |
| <code>feed</code> | <code>ms.selection.feed@r2</code> | <code>variant</code> | <code>""</code>; optional | Selection | CASA feed IDs and feed pair selection.<br><em>Surface:</em> Selects the requested feed IDs or pairs. CASA 6.7.6.14 flagdata silently ignores this selector. |
| <code>clipminmax</code> | <code>flagdata.clipminmax@r1</code> | <code>array&lt;float&gt;</code> | <code>[]</code>; optional | Clip | Two values define the closed clipping range. Other lengths leave the range unset. |
| <code>datacolumn</code> | <code>parameter.datacolumn.flagdata@r1</code> | <code>variant</code> | <code>"DATA"</code>; optional | Data | Visibility data column selected by the clip, tfcrop and rflag agents.<br><em>Surface:</em> Unaveraged TFCrop reads DATA, CORRECTED, MODEL, RESIDUAL, RESIDUAL_DATA, FLOAT_DATA, WEIGHT or WEIGHT_SPECTRUM. DATA and RESIDUAL_DATA use complex DATA when both observed columns are present. A string vector is recorded in HISTORY when the active agent ignores this argument. |
| <code>clipoutside</code> | <code>flagdata.clipoutside@r1</code> | <code>variant</code> | <code>true</code>; optional | Clip | Flag samples outside the closed clip range when true, or inside it when false. |
| <code>channelavg</code> | <code>flagdata.channelavg@r2</code> | <code>variant</code> | <code>false</code>; optional | Clip | Average selected channels before clipping or TFCrop.<br><em>Surface:</em> MeasurementSet TFCrop uses channel averaging. Calibration automatic agents use stored channels. MeasurementSet RFlag channel averaging remains on #978. |
| <code>chanbin</code> | <code>parameter.chanbin@r3</code> | <code>variant</code> | <code>1</code>; optional | Clip | Channel averaging width for clipping or TFCrop.<br><em>Surface:</em> Calibration automatic agents use stored channel samples. |
| <code>timeavg</code> | <code>flagdata.timeavg@r2</code> | <code>variant</code> | <code>false</code>; optional | Clip | Average baseline integrations before clipping or TFCrop.<br><em>Surface:</em> MeasurementSet TFCrop uses time averaging. Calibration automatic agents use stored integrations. MeasurementSet RFlag time averaging remains on #978. |
| <code>timebin</code> | <code>parameter.timebin@r2</code> | <code>string</code> | <code>"0s"</code>; optional | Clip | Averaging time bin, a time quantity as CASA writes it.<br><em>Surface:</em> Calibration automatic agents parse this value when time averaging is requested. |
| <code>clipzeros</code> | <code>parameter.clipzeros@r1</code> | <code>bool</code> | <code>false</code>; optional | Flagging | Flag exact zero amplitudes in clip mode. |
| <code>quackinterval</code> | <code>parameter.quackinterval@r1</code> | <code>variant</code> | <code>1.0</code>; optional | Quack | Quack interval in seconds. |
| <code>quackmode</code> | <code>parameter.quackmode@r2</code> | <code>variant</code> | <code>"beg"</code>; optional | Quack | Which part of each scan quack flags, as CASA names it: 'beg' its first quackinterval, 'endb' its last, 'end' all but its last, 'tail' all but its first. |
| <code>quackincrement</code> | <code>flagdata.quackincrement@r1</code> | <code>variant</code> | <code>false</code>; optional | Quack | Quack successive intervals, taking existing flags into account. |
| <code>tolerance</code> | <code>flagdata.tolerance@r2</code> | <code>variant</code> | <code>0.0</code>; optional | Shadow | Shadowing in metres that shadow mode tolerates. |
| <code>addantenna</code> | <code>flagdata.addantenna@r2</code> | <code>variant</code> | <code>""</code>; optional | Shadow | Antennas, from a file or dictionary, that shadow mode adds. |
| <code>lowerlimit</code> | <code>flagdata.lowerlimit@r2</code> | <code>variant</code> | <code>0.0</code>; optional | Elevation | Lowest elevation, in degrees, that elevation mode keeps. |
| <code>upperlimit</code> | <code>flagdata.upperlimit@r2</code> | <code>variant</code> | <code>90.0</code>; optional | Elevation | Highest elevation, in degrees, that elevation mode keeps. |
| <code>ntime</code> | <code>flagdata.ntime@r2</code> | <code>variant</code> | <code>"scan"</code>; optional | Automatic | Visibility iterator time chunk: 'scan', a duration string, or seconds.<br><em>Surface:</em> Time-averaged clipping, TFCrop and flag extension process each iterator chunk independently. Calibration automatic agents follow stored scan boundaries. MeasurementSet RFlag chunk controls remain on #1616. |
| <code>combinescans</code> | <code>flagdata.combinescans@r1</code> | <code>bool</code> | <code>false</code>; optional | Automatic | Let the automatic modes' time chunks span scans. |
| <code>timecutoff</code> | <code>parameter.timecutoff@r1</code> | <code>float</code> | <code>4.0</code>; optional | Automatic | TFCrop time cutoff in robust sigma units. |
| <code>freqcutoff</code> | <code>parameter.freqcutoff@r1</code> | <code>float</code> | <code>3.0</code>; optional | Automatic | TFCrop frequency cutoff in robust sigma units. |
| <code>timefit</code> | <code>flagdata.timefit@r1</code> | <code>string</code> | <code>"line"</code>; optional | Automatic | Fit tfcrop makes along time: 'line' or 'poly'. |
| <code>freqfit</code> | <code>flagdata.freqfit@r1</code> | <code>string</code> | <code>"poly"</code>; optional | Automatic | Fit tfcrop makes along frequency: 'line' or 'poly'. |
| <code>maxnpieces</code> | <code>flagdata.maxnpieces@r2</code> | <code>variant</code> | <code>7</code>; optional | Automatic | Number of pieces in tfcrop's piecewise polynomial fits. |
| <code>flagdimension</code> | <code>flagdata.flagdimension@r1</code> | <code>string</code> | <code>"freqtime"</code>; optional | Automatic | Directions tfcrop flags along: 'freq', 'time', 'freqtime' or 'timefreq'. |
| <code>usewindowstats</code> | <code>flagdata.usewindowstats@r1</code> | <code>string</code> | <code>"none"</code>; optional | Automatic | Sliding-window statistics tfcrop adds: 'none', 'sum', 'std' or 'both'. |
| <code>halfwin</code> | <code>flagdata.halfwin@r2</code> | <code>variant</code> | <code>1</code>; optional | Automatic | Half-width of tfcrop's sliding window. |
| <code>extendflags</code> | <code>parameter.extendflags@r1</code> | <code>bool</code> | <code>true</code>; optional | Extend | Run post-extension after automatic flagging. |
| <code>winsize</code> | <code>flagdata.winsize@r2</code> | <code>variant</code> | <code>3</code>; optional | Automatic | Number of time steps in rflag's sliding window. |
| <code>timedev</code> | <code>parameter.timedev@r3</code> | <code>optional&lt;variant&gt; (states: , none)</code> | <code>""</code>; optional | Automatic | Explicit RFlag time threshold.<br><em>Surface:</em> Set a scalar, field/SPW/threshold rows or a CASA threshold file. Calculate mode can write the fitted values to a file. |
| <code>freqdev</code> | <code>parameter.freqdev@r3</code> | <code>optional&lt;variant&gt; (states: , none)</code> | <code>""</code>; optional | Automatic | Explicit RFlag spectral threshold.<br><em>Surface:</em> Set a scalar, field/SPW/threshold rows or a CASA threshold file. Calculate mode can write the fitted values to a file. |
| <code>timedevscale</code> | <code>flagdata.timedevscale@r1</code> | <code>float</code> | <code>5.0</code>; optional | Automatic | Factor that scales rflag's time-deviation threshold. |
| <code>freqdevscale</code> | <code>flagdata.freqdevscale@r1</code> | <code>float</code> | <code>5.0</code>; optional | Automatic | Factor that scales rflag's frequency-deviation threshold. |
| <code>spectralmax</code> | <code>flagdata.spectralmax@r1</code> | <code>float</code> | <code>1000000.0</code>; optional | Automatic | rflag flags a whole spectrum whose deviation exceeds this. |
| <code>spectralmin</code> | <code>flagdata.spectralmin@r1</code> | <code>float</code> | <code>0.0</code>; optional | Automatic | rflag flags a whole spectrum whose deviation is below this. |
| <code>antint_ref_antenna</code> | <code>flagdata.antint_ref_antenna@r1</code> | <code>string</code> | <code>""</code>; optional | Antint | Antenna whose baselines antint mode examines. |
| <code>minchanfrac</code> | <code>flagdata.minchanfrac@r2</code> | <code>variant</code> | <code>0.6</code>; optional | Antint | Fraction of flagged channels at which antint flags an integration. |
| <code>verbose</code> | <code>parameter.verbose@r1</code> | <code>bool</code> | <code>false</code>; optional | Antint | Verbose logging. |
| <code>extendpols</code> | <code>parameter.extendpols@r2</code> | <code>variant</code> | <code>true</code>; optional | Extend | Extend flags across correlations. |
| <code>growtime</code> | <code>flagdata.growtime@r2</code> | <code>variant</code> | <code>50.0</code>; optional | Extend | Percentage of flagged time at which extend mode flags a whole channel. |
| <code>growfreq</code> | <code>flagdata.growfreq@r2</code> | <code>variant</code> | <code>50.0</code>; optional | Extend | Percentage of flagged channels at which extend mode flags a whole time step. |
| <code>growaround</code> | <code>flagdata.growaround@r1</code> | <code>bool</code> | <code>false</code>; optional | Extend | Extend flags to points mostly surrounded by flags. |
| <code>flagneartime</code> | <code>flagdata.flagneartime@r1</code> | <code>bool</code> | <code>false</code>; optional | Extend | Flag the time steps either side of each flagged one. |
| <code>flagnearfreq</code> | <code>flagdata.flagnearfreq@r1</code> | <code>bool</code> | <code>false</code>; optional | Extend | Flag the channels either side of each flagged one. |
| <code>minrel</code> | <code>flagdata.minrel@r1</code> | <code>float</code> | <code>0.0</code>; optional | Summary | Smallest flagged fraction a summary entry reports. |
| <code>maxrel</code> | <code>flagdata.maxrel@r1</code> | <code>float</code> | <code>1.0</code>; optional | Summary | Largest flagged fraction a summary entry reports. |
| <code>minabs</code> | <code>flagdata.minabs@r1</code> | <code>integer</code> | <code>0</code>; optional | Summary | Smallest flag count a summary entry reports. |
| <code>maxabs</code> | <code>flagdata.maxabs@r1</code> | <code>integer</code> | <code>-1</code>; optional | Summary | Largest flag count a summary entry reports; -1 for no limit. |
| <code>spwchan</code> | <code>flagdata.spwchan@r1</code> | <code>bool</code> | <code>false</code>; optional | Summary | Report flags per spectral window and channel in the summary. |
| <code>spwcorr</code> | <code>flagdata.spwcorr@r1</code> | <code>bool</code> | <code>false</code>; optional | Summary | Report flags per spectral window and correlation in the summary. |
| <code>basecnt</code> | <code>flagdata.basecnt@r1</code> | <code>bool</code> | <code>false</code>; optional | Summary | Report flags per baseline in the summary. |
| <code>fieldcnt</code> | <code>flagdata.fieldcnt@r1</code> | <code>bool</code> | <code>false</code>; optional | Summary | Break the summary down per field. |
| <code>name</code> | <code>flagdata.name@r1</code> | <code>string</code> | <code>"Summary"</code>; optional | Summary | Name the summary's dictionary carries. |
| <code>action</code> | <code>flagdata.action@r2</code> | <code>choice (4 values)</code> | <code>"apply"</code>; optional | Flagging | CASA's flagdata action: 'apply' writes the flags, 'calculate' computes them without writing, 'none' or '' does neither. |
| <code>display</code> | <code>flagdata.display@r2</code> | <code>string</code> | <code>""</code>; optional | Output | Show data or reports while flagging: '', 'data', 'report' or 'both'.<br><em>Surface:</em> The headless provider applies or calculates flags and returns the task result. A nonempty display suppresses command saving for apply and calculate. Interactive viewing belongs to CASA's plotting backend. |
| <code>flagbackup</code> | <code>parameter.flagbackup@r1</code> | <code>bool</code> | <code>true</code>; optional | Safety | Create a flagmanager backup before mutating flags. |
| <code>savepars</code> | <code>flagdata.savepars@r1</code> | <code>bool</code> | <code>false</code>; optional | Output | Save the flag commands to FLAG_CMD or to outfile. |
| <code>cmdreason</code> | <code>flagdata.cmdreason@r1</code> | <code>string</code> | <code>""</code>; optional | Output | Reason saved with the flag commands. |
| <code>outfile</code> | <code>savepars.outfile@r1</code> | <code>string</code> | <code>""</code>; optional | Output | File that receives the saved flag commands; empty for the FLAG_CMD table. |
| <code>overwrite</code> | <code>savepars.overwrite@r1</code> | <code>bool</code> | <code>true</code>; optional | Output | Let savepars replace an existing command file. |
| <code>writeflags</code> | <code>flagdata.writeflags@r1</code> | <code>bool</code> | <code>true</code>; optional | Output | Internal flag-write argument. Flagdata derives persistence from action. |

| <code>outfile</code> | <code>parameter.outfile@r1</code> | <code>path (image)</code> | <code>""</code>; required | Output | Output image. |
| <code>mode</code> | <code>impv.mode@r2</code> | <code>string</code> | <code>"coords"</code>; optional | Slice | How the slice is given: by start and end, or by center, length and position angle. |
| <code>start</code> | <code>parameter.start@r2</code> | <code>variant</code> | <code>""</code>; optional | Slice | Cube-axis start channel, frequency, or velocity |

| <code>stokes</code> | <code>image.selection.stokes@r1</code> | <code>string</code> | <code>""</code>; optional | Selection | Stokes selector. |
| <code>mask</code> | <code>parameter.mask@r1</code> | <code>string</code> | <code>""</code>; optional | Selection | Mask expression. |
| <code>stretch</code> | <code>parameter.stretch@r2</code> | <code>bool</code> | <code>false</code>; optional | Selection | Stretch the mask along the image's other axes if necessary and possible. |

## Image Math (<code>immath</code>)

- Kind: `task`
- Contract version: `5`
- Category: Images
- Provider family: `image_analysis`
- Summary: Evaluate mathematical expressions and spectral or polarization operations on images

| Parameter | Concept ID / revision | Type / unit | Default / required | Group | Summary |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| <code>imagename</code> | <code>image.input.imagename@r1</code> | <code>array&lt;path (image)&gt;</code> | <code>""</code>; optional | Input | Input CASA image path |
| <code>mode</code> | <code>immath.mode@r1</code> | <code>choice (6 values)</code> | <code>"evalexpr"</code>; optional | Math | Image math: an expression, a spectral index, or a polarization angle or intensity. |
| <code>outfile</code> | <code>parameter.outfile@r1</code> | <code>path (image)</code> | <code>"immath_results.im"</code>; optional | Output | Output image. |
| <code>expr</code> | <code>parameter.expr@r1</code> | <code>string</code> | <code>"IM0"</code>; optional | Math | Expression such as IM0 * IM1 or IM0 / IM1 |
| <code>varnames</code> | <code>immath.varnames@r1</code> | <code>array&lt;string&gt;</code> | <code>""</code>; optional | Math | Names the expression gives the input images, in their order. |
| <code>sigma</code> | <code>immath.sigma@r1</code> | <code>string</code> | <code>"0.0mJy/beam"</code>; optional | Polarization | Noise standard deviation for debiasing polarized intensity. |
| <code>polithresh</code> | <code>immath.polithresh@r1</code> | <code>string</code> | <code>""</code>; optional | Polarization | Linearly polarized intensity below which the polarization angle masks pixels. |
| <code>mask</code> | <code>parameter.mask@r1</code> | <code>string</code> | <code>""</code>; optional | Selection | Mask expression. |
| <code>region</code> | <code>image.selection.region@r1</code> | <code>string</code> | <code>""</code>; optional | Selection | Region file or expression. |
| <code>box</code> | <code>image.selection.box@r1</code> | <code>string</code> | <code>""</code>; optional | Selection | Pixel box. |
| <code>chans</code> | <code>image.selection.chans@r1</code> | <code>string</code> | <code>""</code>; optional | Selection | Channel selector. |
| <code>stokes</code> | <code>image.selection.stokes@r1</code> | <code>string</code> | <code>""</code>; optional | Selection | Stokes selector. |
| <code>stretch</code> | <code>parameter.stretch@r2</code> | <code>bool</code> | <code>false</code>; optional | Selection | Stretch the mask along the image's other axes if necessary and possible. |
| <code>imagemd</code> | <code>immath.imagemd@r1</code> | <code>string</code> | <code>""</code>; optional | Output | Image whose header metadata the output copies. |
| <code>prec</code> | <code>immath.prec@r1</code> | <code>string</code> | <code>"float"</code>; optional | Output | Output pixel precision for evalexpr and spix: float or double, minimum match. |
| <code>overwrite</code> | <code>output.overwrite@r1</code> | <code>bool</code> | <code>false</code>; optional | Output | Replace an existing output file<br><em>Surface:</em> casa-rs's own control: CASA's immath has no overwrite parameter. |

| <code>replicate</code> | <code>imregrid.replicate@r1</code> | <code>bool</code> | <code>false</code>; optional | Regrid | Replicate the image rather than regrid it. |

<a id="surface-msconvert"></a>

## MS Convert (<code>msconvert</code>)

- Kind: `task`
- Contract version: `1`
- Category: MeasurementSet
- Provider family: `msconvert`
- Summary: Convert between MeasurementSet v2 and MSv4, in either direction.

| Parameter | Concept ID / revision | Type / unit | Default / required | Group | Summary |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| <code>vis</code> | <code>data.input.vis@r1</code> | <code>array&lt;path (measurement_set)&gt;</code> | <code>""</code>; required | Input | Input MeasurementSet path or paths. |
| <code>outputvis</code> | <code>parameter.outputvis@r1</code> | <code>path (measurement_set)</code> | <code>""</code>; optional | Output | Output MeasurementSet path.<br><em>Surface:</em> Named as XRADIO names a processing set: an MSv4 output not ending in ps.zarr gains .ps.zarr. When outputvis is empty, msconvert writes beside vis, naming the output after it, its .ms, .zarr or .ps.zarr replaced by .ps.zarr for an MSv4 or .ms for an MSv2. |
| <code>outformat</code> | <code>msconvert.outformat@r1</code> | <code>choice (3 values)</code> | <code>""</code>; optional | Output | Format of the converted MeasurementSet: msv2 or msv4. |
| <code>datagroups</code> | <code>msconvert.datagroups@r1</code> | <code>array&lt;string&gt;</code> | <code>[]</code>; optional | Data | Data groups to convert; empty converts every one. |
| <code>partitionscheme</code> | <code>msconvert.partitionscheme@r1</code> | <code>array&lt;choice (2 values)&gt;</code> | <code>[]</code>; optional | MSv4 Output | MSv2 into MSv4: further keys the MSv2 is partitioned by, as XRADIO's partition_scheme. |
| <code>withpointing</code> | <code>msconvert.withpointing@r1</code> | <code>bool</code> | <code>true</code>; optional | MSv4 Output | MSv4 output: convert the pointing, as XRADIO's with_pointing. |
| <code>padding</code> | <code>msconvert.padding@r1</code> | <code>choice (2 values)</code> | <code>"flagged"</code>; optional | MSv2 Output | MSv2 output: what a grid cell no acquisition filled becomes. |
| <code>chunks</code> | <code>msconvert.chunks@r1</code> | <code>array&lt;integer&gt;</code> | <code>[]</code>; optional | MSv4 Output | MSv4 output: chunk shape [time, baseline, frequency, polarization]; empty for XRADIO's default. |
| <code>codec</code> | <code>msconvert.codec@r1</code> | <code>choice (3 values)</code> | <code>"blosc"</code>; optional | MSv4 Output | MSv4 output: compression of the visibilities, flags and weights. |
| <code>nthreads</code> | <code>workers.msconvert@r1</code> | <code>integer</code> | <code>0</code>; optional | MSv4 Output | MSv4 output: partitions converted at once; 0 for as many as the cores and the memory allow. |
| <code>overwrite</code> | <code>output.overwrite@r1</code> | <code>bool</code> | <code>false</code>; optional | Output | Replace an existing output file |

## Get ALMA Antenna Positions (<code>getantposalma</code>)

- Kind: `task`
- Contract version: `1`
- Category: Calibration
- Provider family: `calibration`
- Summary: Retrieve antenna positions from the ALMA archive.

| Parameter | Concept ID / revision | Type / unit | Default / required | Group | Summary |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| <code>outfile</code> | <code>positions.output.outfile@r1</code> | <code>path (file)</code> | <code>""</code>; optional | Output | JSON file for the retrieved antenna positions. |
| <code>overwrite</code> | <code>output.overwrite@r1</code> | <code>bool</code> | <code>false</code>; optional | Output | Replace an existing output file |
| <code>asdm</code> | <code>getantposalma.asdm@r1</code> | <code>string</code> | <code>""</code>; optional | ALMA Service | ASDM UID passed to the ALMA antenna-position service. |
| <code>tw</code> | <code>getantposalma.tw@r1</code> | <code>string</code> | <code>""</code>; optional | ALMA Service | Optional pair of UTC timestamps defining the measurement window. |
| <code>snr</code> | <code>getantposalma.snr@r1</code> | <code>variant</code> | <code>"default"</code>; optional | ALMA Service | Minimum signal-to-noise ratio or the service default. |
| <code>search</code> | <code>getantposalma.search@r1</code> | <code>string</code> | <code>"both_latest"</code>; optional | ALMA Service | Search algorithm sent to the ALMA service. |
| <code>hosts</code> | <code>getantposalma.hosts@r1</code> | <code>array&lt;string&gt;</code> | <code>["https://asa.alma.cl/uncertainties-service/uncertainties/versions/last/measurements/casa/"]</code>; optional | ALMA Service | ALMA service URLs in fallback order. |
| <code>firstintegration</code> | <code>getantposalma.firstintegration@r1</code> | <code>bool</code> | <code>true</code>; optional | ALMA Service | Exclude measurements flagged as first integrations. |
| <code>nretry</code> | <code>getantposalma.nretry@r1</code> | <code>integer</code> | <code>0</code>; optional | ALMA Service | Number of retries after the initial round of service requests. |
| <code>rdelay</code> | <code>getantposalma.rdelay@r1</code> | <code>float</code> | <code>3.0</code>; optional | ALMA Service | Seconds between retries. |

<a id="surface-rerefant"></a>

## Re-reference Calibration Solutions (<code>rerefant</code>)

- Kind: `task`
- Contract version: `1`
- Category: Calibration
- Provider family: `calibration`
- Summary: Change the reference antenna in a calibration table.

| Parameter | Concept ID / revision | Type / unit | Default / required | Group | Summary |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| <code>vis</code> | <code>data.input.vis@r3</code> | <code>path (measurement_set)</code> | <code>""</code>; required | Input | One input MeasurementSet path. |
| <code>tablein</code> | <code>calibration.tablein@r1</code> | <code>path (calibration_table)</code> | <code>""</code>; required | Input | Existing input calibration table. |
| <code>caltable</code> | <code>calibration.caltable@r2</code> | <code>path (calibration_table)</code> | <code>""</code>; optional | Output | Calibration table the task writes. |
| <code>refantmode</code> | <code>setsolve.refantmode@r1</code> | <code>choice (2 values)</code> | <code>"flex"</code>; optional | Reference antenna | Whether another reference antenna is used where refant has no solution. |
| <code>refant</code> | <code>parameter.refant@r1</code> | <code>string</code> | <code>""</code>; optional | Reference antenna | Reference antenna name or id for solve workflows |

| <code>plotName</code> | <code>calibration.weather_plot_name@r1</code> | <code>string</code> | <code>""</code>; optional | Plot | Name of the weather summary plot file. |

<a id="surface-initweights"></a>

## Initialize Visibility Weights (<code>initweights</code>)

- Kind: `task`
- Contract version: `1`

- Summary: Initialize MeasurementSet weights and their channel spectra.

| Parameter | Concept ID / revision | Type / unit | Default / required | Group | Summary |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| <code>vis</code> | <code>data.input.vis@r1</code> | <code>array&lt;path (measurement_set)&gt;</code> | <code>""</code>; required | Input | Input MeasurementSet path or paths. |
| <code>wtmode</code> | <code>weightinit.wtmode@r1</code> | <code>choice (8 values)</code> | <code>"nyq"</code>; optional | Weights | Method used to initialize the visibility weights. |
| <code>tsystable</code> | <code>weightinit.tsystable@r1</code> | <code>path (calibration_table)</code> | <code>""</code>; optional | Weights | Tsys calibration table used by the Tsys weight modes. |
| <code>gainfield</code> | <code>weightinit.gainfield@r1</code> | <code>string</code> | <code>""</code>; optional | Weights | Select solutions from this field in the Tsys table. |
| <code>interp</code> | <code>weightinit.interp@r1</code> | <code>string</code> | <code>""</code>; optional | Weights | Interpolate the Tsys solutions in time and frequency. |
| <code>spwmap</code> | <code>weightinit.spwmap@r1</code> | <code>array&lt;integer&gt;</code> | <code>[]</code>; optional | Weights | Map data spectral windows to Tsys solution windows. |
| <code>dowtsp</code> | <code>weightinit.dowtsp@r1</code> | <code>bool</code> | <code>false</code>; optional | Weights | Create WEIGHT_SPECTRUM when it is absent. |

<a id="surface-statwt"></a>

## Statistical Weights (<code>statwt</code>)

- Kind: `task`
- Contract version: `1`
- Category: Calibration
- Provider family: `calibration`
- Summary: Estimate MeasurementSet weights from visibility variance.

| Parameter | Concept ID / revision | Type / unit | Default / required | Group | Summary |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| <code>vis</code> | <code>data.input.vis@r1</code> | <code>array&lt;path (measurement_set)&gt;</code> | <code>""</code>; required | Input | Input MeasurementSet path or paths. |
| <code>selectdata</code> | <code>parameter.selectdata@r1</code> | <code>bool</code> | <code>true</code>; optional | Weights | Whether detailed data-selection controls are enabled. |
| <code>field</code> | <code>ms.selection.field@r4</code> | <code>string</code> | <code>""</code>; optional | Weights | CASA field selector text. Empty selects every field. |
| <code>spw</code> | <code>ms.selection.spw@r3</code> | <code>string</code> | <code>""</code>; optional | Weights | Applycal spw selector text. |
| <code>intent</code> | <code>ms.selection.intent@r3</code> | <code>string</code> | <code>""</code>; optional | Weights | Intent selector. |
| <code>array</code> | <code>ms.selection.array@r1</code> | <code>string</code> | <code>""</code>; optional | Weights | Comma-separated ARRAY_ID integers |
| <code>observation</code> | <code>ms.selection.observation@r3</code> | <code>string</code> | <code>""</code>; optional | Weights | Observation selection applied to every MeasurementSet |
| <code>scan</code> | <code>ms.selection.scan@r4</code> | <code>string</code> | <code>""</code>; optional | Weights | Applycal scan selector text. |
| <code>combine</code> | <code>weightstats.combine@r1</code> | <code>string</code> | <code>""</code>; optional | Weights | Ignore changes in these columns (scan, field, and/or state) when aggregating samples to compute weights. The value "corr" is also supported to aggregate samples across correlations. |
| <code>timebin</code> | <code>weightstats.timebin@r1</code> | <code>variant</code> | <code>1</code>; optional | Weights | Length for binning in time to determine statistics. Can either be integer to be multiplied by the representative integration time, a quantity (string) in time units |
| <code>slidetimebin</code> | <code>weightstats.slidetimebin@r1</code> | <code>bool</code> | <code>false</code>; optional | Weights | Use a sliding window for time binning, as opposed to time block processing? |
| <code>chanbin</code> | <code>weightstats.chanbin@r1</code> | <code>variant</code> | <code>"spw"</code>; optional | Weights | Channel bin width for computing weights. Can either be integer, in which case it is interpreted as number of channels to include in each bin, or a string "spw" or quantity with frequency units. |
| <code>minsamp</code> | <code>weightstats.minsamp@r1</code> | <code>integer</code> | <code>2</code>; optional | Weights | Minimum number of unflagged visibilities required for computing weights in a sample. Must be &gt;= 2. |
| <code>statalg</code> | <code>weightstats.statalg@r1</code> | <code>string</code> | <code>"classic"</code>; optional | Weights | Statistics algorithm to use for computing variances. Supported values are "chauvenet", "classic", "fit-half", and "hinges-fences". Minimum match is supported, although the full string must be specified for the subparameters to appear in the inputs list. |
| <code>fence</code> | <code>weightstats.fence@r1</code> | <code>float</code> | <code>-1.0</code>; optional | Weights | Fence value for statalg="hinges-fences". A negative value means use the entire data set (ie default to the "classic" algorithm). Ignored if statalg is not "hinges-fences". |
| <code>center</code> | <code>weightstats.center@r1</code> | <code>string</code> | <code>"mean"</code>; optional | Weights | Center to use for statalg="fit-half". Valid choices are "mean", "median", and "zero". Ignored if statalg is not "fit-half". |
| <code>lside</code> | <code>weightstats.lside@r1</code> | <code>bool</code> | <code>true</code>; optional | Weights | For statalg="fit-half", real data are &lt;=; center? If false, real data are &gt;= center. Ignored if statalg is not "fit-half". |
| <code>zscore</code> | <code>weightstats.zscore@r1</code> | <code>float</code> | <code>-1.0</code>; optional | Weights | For statalg="chauvenet", this is the target maximum number of standard deviations data may have to be included. If negative, use Chauvenet's criterion. Ignored if statalg is not "chauvenet". |
| <code>maxiter</code> | <code>weightstats.maxiter@r1</code> | <code>integer</code> | <code>-1</code>; optional | Weights | For statalg="chauvenet", this is the maximum number of iterations to attempt. Iterating will stop when either this limit is reached, or the zscore criterion is met. If negative, iterate until the zscore criterion is met. Ignored if statalg is not "chauvenet". |
| <code>fitspw</code> | <code>weightstats.fitspw@r1</code> | <code>string</code> | <code>""</code>; optional | Weights | Channels to include in the computation of weights. Specified as an MS select channel selection string. |
| <code>excludechans</code> | <code>weightstats.excludechans@r1</code> | <code>bool</code> | <code>false</code>; optional | Weights | If True: invert the channel selection in fitspw and exclude the fitspw selection from the computation of the weights. |
| <code>wtrange</code> | <code>weightstats.wtrange@r1</code> | <code>array&lt;float&gt;</code> | <code>[]</code>; optional | Weights | Range of acceptable weights. Data with weights outside this range will be flagged. Empty array (default) means all weights are good. |
| <code>flagbackup</code> | <code>parameter.flagbackup@r1</code> | <code>bool</code> | <code>true</code>; optional | Weights | Create a flagmanager backup before mutating flags. |
| <code>preview</code> | <code>weightstats.preview@r1</code> | <code>bool</code> | <code>false</code>; optional | Weights | Preview mode. If True, no data is changed, although the amount of data that would have been flagged is reported. |
| <code>datacolumn</code> | <code>weightstats.datacolumn@r1</code> | <code>string</code> | <code>"corrected"</code>; optional | Weights | Data column to use to compute weights. Supported values are "data", "corrected", "residual", and "residual_data" (case insensitive, minimum match supported). |

| <code>datacolumn</code> | <code>statistics.calibration_datacolumn@r1</code> | <code>string</code> | <code>"gain"</code>; optional | Statistics | Column to summarize for amplitude, phase, real or imaginary statistics. |

| Parameter | Concept ID / revision | Type / unit | Default / required | Group | Summary |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|

| <code>caltable</code> | <code>parameter.caltable@r1</code> | <code>path (calibration_table)</code> | <code>""</code>; required | Input | Input calibration table. |
| <code>field</code> | <code>ms.selection.field@r4</code> | <code>string</code> | <code>""</code>; optional | Selection | CASA field selector text. Empty selects every field. |
| <code>antenna</code> | <code>ms.selection.antenna@r4</code> | <code>string</code> | <code>""</code>; optional | Selection | Applycal antenna selector text. |

| <code>listfile</code> | <code>statistics.listing_file@r1</code> | <code>path (file)</code> | <code>""</code>; optional | Output | Write the solution listing to a new text file. Empty writes to the terminal. |
| <code>pagerows</code> | <code>statistics.listing_pagerows@r1</code> | <code>integer</code> | <code>50</code>; optional | Output | Maximum rows per page. Zero or a negative value prints a continuous listing. |

<a id="surface-plotbandpass"></a>

## Plot Bandpass (<code>plotbandpass</code>)

- Kind: `task`
- Contract version: `1`
- Category: Calibration
- Provider family: `calibration`
- Summary: Plot calibration spectra and channel derivatives.

| Parameter | Concept ID / revision | Type / unit | Default / required | Group | Summary |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| <code>caltable</code> | <code>parameter.caltable@r1</code> | <code>path (calibration_table)</code> | <code>""</code>; required | Input | Input calibration table. |
| <code>antenna</code> | <code>ms.selection.antenna@r5</code> | <code>variant</code> | <code>""</code>; optional | Plot | A comma-delimited string list of antennas (either names or integer indices) for which to display solutions. Default = all antennas. |
| <code>field</code> | <code>ms.selection.field@r6</code> | <code>variant</code> | <code>""</code>; optional | Plot | A comma-delimited string list of fields (either names or integer indices) for which to display solutions. Default = all fields. |
| <code>spw</code> | <code>ms.selection.spw@r4</code> | <code>variant</code> | <code>""</code>; optional | Plot | A comma-delimited string list of spws for which to display solutions. Default = all spws. |
| <code>yaxis</code> | <code>plotbandpass.yaxis@r1</code> | <code>string</code> | <code>"amp"</code>; optional | Plot | The quantity to plot on the y-axis ("amp", "phase", "both", "tsys", append "db" for dB). |
| <code>xaxis</code> | <code>plotbandpass.xaxis@r1</code> | <code>string</code> | <code>"chan"</code>; optional | Plot | The quantity to plot on the x-axis ("chan" or "freq"). |
| <code>figfile</code> | <code>plotbandpass.figfile@r1</code> | <code>string</code> | <code>""</code>; optional | Plot | Base name used to derive each saved plot and PDF name. |
| <code>plotrange</code> | <code>plotbandpass.plotrange@r1</code> | <code>array&lt;float&gt;</code> | <code>[0.0,0.0,0.0,0.0]</code>; optional | Plot | The axes limits to use [x0,x1,y0,y1]. |
| <code>caltable2</code> | <code>plotbandpass.caltable2@r1</code> | <code>string</code> | <code>""</code>; optional | Input | A second cal table, of type BPOLY or B, to overlay on a B table |
| <code>overlay</code> | <code>plotbandpass.overlay@r1</code> | <code>string</code> | <code>""</code>; optional | Plot | Show multiple solutions in same frame in different colors (time, antenna, spw, baseband, or time,antenna) |
| <code>showflagged</code> | <code>plotbandpass.showflagged@r1</code> | <code>bool</code> | <code>false</code>; optional | Plot | Show the values of the solution, even if flagged |
| <code>timeranges</code> | <code>plotbandpass.timeranges@r1</code> | <code>string</code> | <code>""</code>; optional | Plot | Show only these timeranges, the first timerange being 0 |
| <code>buildpdf</code> | <code>plotbandpass.buildpdf@r1</code> | <code>bool</code> | <code>false</code>; optional | Plot | If True, assemble all the pngs into a pdf |
| <code>caltable3</code> | <code>plotbandpass.caltable3@r1</code> | <code>string</code> | <code>""</code>; optional | Input | A third cal table, of type BPOLY, to overlay on the first two tables |
| <code>markersize</code> | <code>plotbandpass.markersize@r1</code> | <code>integer</code> | <code>3</code>; optional | Plot | Size of points |
| <code>density</code> | <code>plotbandpass.density@r1</code> | <code>integer</code> | <code>108</code>; optional | Plot | dpi to use in creating PNGs and PDFs (default=108) |
| <code>interactive</code> | <code>parameter.interactive@r1</code> | <code>bool</code> | <code>true</code>; optional | Plot | Interactive clean. |
| <code>showpoints</code> | <code>plotbandpass.showpoints@r1</code> | <code>variant</code> | <code>"auto"</code>; optional | Plot | Draw points for the data (default=F for amp, T for phase) |
| <code>showlines</code> | <code>plotbandpass.showlines@r1</code> | <code>variant</code> | <code>"auto"</code>; optional | Plot | Draw lines connecting the data (default=T for amp, F for phase) |
| <code>subplot</code> | <code>plotbandpass.subplot@r1</code> | <code>variant</code> | <code>"22"</code>; optional | Plot | 11..81,22,32 or 42 for RowsxColumns (default=22), any 3rd digit is ignored |
| <code>zoom</code> | <code>plotbandpass.zoom@r1</code> | <code>string</code> | <code>""</code>; optional | Plot | "intersect" will zoom to overlap region of caltable with caltable2 |
| <code>poln</code> | <code>plotbandpass.poln@r1</code> | <code>variant</code> | <code>""</code>; optional | Plot | Polarizations to plot: "" = all, or "RR","RL","LR","LL","XX","XY","YX","YY","RR,LL","XX,YY" |
| <code>showatm</code> | <code>plotbandpass.showatm@r1</code> | <code>bool</code> | <code>false</code>; optional | Plot | Compute and overlay the atmospheric transmission curve |
| <code>pwv</code> | <code>visualization.pwv@r1</code> | <code>variant</code> | <code>"auto"</code>; optional | Plot | Define the pwv to use for the showatm option: "auto" or value in mm |
| <code>gs</code> | <code>plotbandpass.gs@r1</code> | <code>string</code> | <code>"gs"</code>; optional | Plot | For buildpdf=T, full path for ghostscript command (in case it is not found) |
| <code>convert</code> | <code>plotbandpass.convert@r1</code> | <code>string</code> | <code>"convert"</code>; optional | Plot | For buildpdf=T, full path for the ImageMagick convert command (in case it is not found) |
| <code>chanrange</code> | <code>plotbandpass.chanrange@r1</code> | <code>variant</code> | <code>""</code>; optional | Plot | Set xrange ("5~100") over which to autoscale y-axis for xaxis="freq" |
| <code>solutionTimeThresholdSeconds</code> | <code>plotbandpass.solution_time_threshold_seconds@r1</code> | <code>variant</code> | <code>30.0</code>; optional | Plot | Consider 2 solutions simultaneous if within this interval in seconds |
| <code>debug</code> | <code>plotbandpass.debug@r1</code> | <code>bool</code> | <code>false</code>; optional | Plot | Print verbose messages for debugging purposes |
| <code>phase</code> | <code>plotbandpass.phase@r1</code> | <code>variant</code> | <code>""</code>; optional | Plot | The y-axis limits to use for phase plots when yaxis="both" |
| <code>vis</code> | <code>data.input.vis@r3</code> | <code>path (measurement_set)</code> | <code>""</code>; optional | Input | One input MeasurementSet path. |
| <code>showtsky</code> | <code>plotbandpass.showtsky@r1</code> | <code>bool</code> | <code>false</code>; optional | Plot | Compute and overlay the sky temperature curve instead of transmission |
| <code>showfdm</code> | <code>plotbandpass.showfdm@r1</code> | <code>bool</code> | <code>false</code>; optional | Plot | when showing TDM spws, draw the locations of the corresponding FDM spws |
| <code>showatmfield</code> | <code>plotbandpass.showatmfield@r1</code> | <code>variant</code> | <code>""</code>; optional | Plot | for overlay="time", use first observation of this fieldID or name |
| <code>lo1</code> | <code>plotbandpass.lo1@r1</code> | <code>variant</code> | <code>""</code>; optional | Plot | specify the LO1 setting (in GHz) for the observation ('' = automatic) |
| <code>showimage</code> | <code>plotbandpass.showimage@r1</code> | <code>bool</code> | <code>false</code>; optional | Plot | also show the atmospheric curve for the image sideband (in black) |
| <code>showatmpoints</code> | <code>plotbandpass.showatmpoints@r1</code> | <code>bool</code> | <code>false</code>; optional | Plot | Draw atmospheric curve with points instead of a line |
| <code>parentms</code> | <code>plotbandpass.parentms@r1</code> | <code>string</code> | <code>""</code>; optional | Plot | if showimage=T, name of the parent ms (only needed if the ms has been previously split) |
| <code>pdftk</code> | <code>plotbandpass.pdftk@r1</code> | <code>string</code> | <code>"pdftk"</code>; optional | Plot | For buildpdf=T, full path for pdftk command (in case it is not found) |
| <code>channeldiff</code> | <code>plotbandpass.channeldiff@r1</code> | <code>variant</code> | <code>false</code>; optional | Plot | Set to a value &gt; 0 (sigma) to plot derivatives of the solutions |
| <code>edge</code> | <code>plotbandpass.edge@r1</code> | <code>integer</code> | <code>8</code>; optional | Plot | The number of edge channels to ignore in finding outliers (for channeldiff&gt;0) |
| <code>resample</code> | <code>plotbandpass.resample@r1</code> | <code>integer</code> | <code>1</code>; optional | Plot | The channel expansion factor to use when computing MAD of derivative (for channeldiff&gt;0) |
| <code>platformingThreshold</code> | <code>plotbandpass.platforming_threshold@r1</code> | <code>variant</code> | <code>10.0</code>; optional | Plot | if platformingSigma=0, then declare platforming if the amplitude derivative exceeds this percentage of the median |
| <code>platformingSigma</code> | <code>plotbandpass.platforming_sigma@r1</code> | <code>variant</code> | <code>10.0</code>; optional | Plot | declare platforming if the amplitude derivative exceeds this many times the MAD |
| <code>basebands</code> | <code>plotbandpass.basebands@r1</code> | <code>variant</code> | <code>""</code>; optional | Plot | A baseband number or list of baseband numbers for which to display solutions. Default = all. |
| <code>showBasebandNumber</code> | <code>plotbandpass.show_baseband_number@r1</code> | <code>bool</code> | <code>false</code>; optional | Plot | Put the baseband converter number (BBC_NO) in the title of each plot |
| <code>scans</code> | <code>plotbandpass.scans@r1</code> | <code>variant</code> | <code>""</code>; optional | Plot | A scan or list of scans for which to display solutions. Default = all. Does not work with overlay="time". |
| <code>figfileSequential</code> | <code>plotbandpass.figfile_sequential@r1</code> | <code>bool</code> | <code>false</code>; optional | Plot | naming scheme for pngs: False: name by spw/antenna (default), True: figfile.000.png, figfile.001.png, etc. |
| <code>chanrangeSetXrange</code> | <code>plotbandpass.chanrange_set_xrange@r1</code> | <code>bool</code> | <code>false</code>; optional | Plot | If True, then chanrange also sets the xrange to display |

<a id="surface-getephemtable"></a>

## Get Ephemeris Table (<code>getephemtable</code>)

- Kind: `task`
- Contract version: `1`
- Category: Calibration
- Provider family: `calibration`
