| Parameter | Concept ID / revision | Type / unit | Default / required | Group | Summary |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| <code>objectname</code> | <code>ephemeris.object_name@r1</code> | <code>string</code> | <code>""</code>; optional | Input | Name or Horizons designation of the target. |
| <code>asis</code> | <code>ephemeris.as_is@r1</code> | <code>bool</code> | <code>false</code>; optional | Input | Pass the supplied designation directly to Horizons. |
| <code>timerange</code> | <code>solar_system.ephemeris.time_range@r1</code> | <code>string</code> | <code>""</code>; optional | Input | Start and stop times separated by a tilde, in calendar, JD or MJD form. |
| <code>interval</code> | <code>ephemeris.interval@r1</code> | <code>string</code> | <code>"5m"</code>; optional | Input | Integer sample count or integer duration with a Horizons unit. |
| <code>outfile</code> | <code>solar_system.ephemeris.output_table@r1</code> | <code>path (table)</code> | <code>""</code>; optional | Output | Write the MeasComet ephemeris table at this path. |
| <code>rawdatafile</code> | <code>ephemeris.raw_output@r1</code> | <code>path (file)</code> | <code>""</code>; optional | Output | Optionally write the original Horizons response text. |
| <code>overwrite</code> | <code>output.overwrite@r1</code> | <code>bool</code> | <code>false</code>; optional | Output | Replace an existing output file |

<a id="surface-getcalmodvla"></a>

## Get VLA Calibrator Model (<code>getcalmodvla</code>)

- Kind: `task`
- Contract version: `1`
- Category: Calibration
- Provider family: `calibration`
- Summary: Retrieve a calibrator brightness model from the VLA service.

| Parameter | Concept ID / revision | Type / unit | Default / required | Group | Summary |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| <code>outfile</code> | <code>vla_calibrator.outfile@r1</code> | <code>path (table)</code> | <code>""</code>; optional | Output | Write the retrieved source model as a CASA component-list table. |
| <code>overwrite</code> | <code>output.overwrite@r1</code> | <code>bool</code> | <code>false</code>; optional | Output | Replace an existing output file |
| <code>source</code> | <code>vla_calibrator.source@r1</code> | <code>string</code> | <code>""</code>; optional | VLA Service | Calibrator name. Specify either the name or the direction. |
| <code>direction</code> | <code>vla_calibrator.direction@r1</code> | <code>string</code> | <code>""</code>; optional | VLA Service | Direction in FRAME LONGITUDE LATITUDE form. |
| <code>band</code> | <code>vla_calibrator.band@r1</code> | <code>string</code> | <code>""</code>; optional | VLA Service | Case-insensitive VLA receiver band P, L, S, C, X, U, K, A or Q. |
| <code>obsdate</code> | <code>vla_calibrator.obsdate@r1</code> | <code>variant</code> | <code>0</code>; optional | VLA Service | Observation date as numeric MJD or a YYYY-MM-DD calendar date. |
| <code>refdate</code> | <code>vla_calibrator.refdate@r1</code> | <code>variant</code> | <code>0</code>; optional | VLA Service | Optional reference date as numeric MJD or a YYYY-MM-DD calendar date. |
| <code>hosts</code> | <code>vla_calibrator.hosts@r1</code> | <code>array&lt;string&gt;</code> | <code>["http://obs.vla.nrao.edu/calmodvla"]</code>; optional | VLA Service | VLA calibrator-service URLs in the order they will be queried. |

<a id="surface-predictcomp"></a>

## Predict Calibrator Component (<code>predictcomp</code>)

- Kind: `task`
- Contract version: `1`
- Category: Calibration
- Provider family: `simulation`
- Summary: Write a flux calibrator component list and optionally plot its visibility amplitudes.

| Parameter | Concept ID / revision | Type / unit | Default / required | Group | Summary |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| <code>objname</code> | <code>predictcomp.objname@r1</code> | <code>string</code> | <code>""</code>; optional | Prediction | Object to predict, using a name recognized by the flux standard. |
| <code>standard</code> | <code>setjy.standard@r1</code> | <code>choice (9 values; surface-narrowed)</code> | <code>"Butler-JPL-Horizons 2010"</code>; optional | Prediction | Flux density standard for the calibrator model. |
| <code>epoch</code> | <code>predictcomp.epoch@r1</code> | <code>string</code> | <code>""</code>; optional | Prediction | UTC observation epoch. Use a CASA calendar date or a quantity in days. |
| <code>minfreq</code> | <code>predictcomp.minfreq@r1</code> | <code>string</code> | <code>""</code>; optional | Prediction | First prediction frequency. Supply a quantity such as 90GHz. |
| <code>maxfreq</code> | <code>predictcomp.maxfreq@r1</code> | <code>string</code> | <code>""</code>; optional | Prediction | Last prediction frequency. An omitted or nonpositive frequency uses minfreq. |
| <code>nfreqs</code> | <code>predictcomp.nfreqs@r1</code> | <code>integer</code> | <code>2</code>; optional | Prediction | Number of evenly spaced frequencies. A range uses at least two samples. |
| <code>prefix</code> | <code>predictcomp.prefix@r1</code> | <code>string</code> | <code>""</code>; optional | Prediction | Prefix prepended to the generated component-list name. |
| <code>antennalist</code> | <code>parameter.antennalist@r1</code> | <code>string</code> | <code>""</code>; optional | Plot | Antenna config list. |
| <code>showplot</code> | <code>predictcomp.showplot@r1</code> | <code>bool</code> | <code>false</code>; optional | Plot | Display the visibility-amplitude figure when an antenna configuration is provided. |
| <code>savefig</code> | <code>predictcomp.savefig@r1</code> | <code>string</code> | <code>""</code>; optional | Plot | Save the visibility-amplitude figure to this path. |
| <code>symb</code> | <code>predictcomp.symb@r1</code> | <code>string</code> | <code>"."</code>; optional | Plot | Matplotlib marker or format string used for each series. |
| <code>include0amp</code> | <code>predictcomp.include0amp@r1</code> | <code>bool</code> | <code>false</code>; optional | Plot | Start the visibility-amplitude axis at zero. |
| <code>include0bl</code> | <code>predictcomp.include0bl@r1</code> | <code>bool</code> | <code>false</code>; optional | Plot | Start the projected-baseline axis at zero. |
| <code>blunit</code> | <code>predictcomp.blunit@r1</code> | <code>choice (2 values)</code> | <code>""</code>; optional | Plot | Display projected lengths in metres by default, or in kilolambda at the first frequency. |
| <code>showbl0flux</code> | <code>predictcomp.showbl0flux@r1</code> | <code>bool</code> | <code>false</code>; optional | Plot | Include the zero-baseline flux in the legend. |

<a id="surface-wvrgcal"></a>

## Water-vapour Radiometer Calibration (<code>wvrgcal</code>)

- Kind: `task`
- Contract version: `1`
- Category: Calibration
- Provider family: `calibration`
- Summary: Use ALMA radiometer measurements to calibrate atmospheric phase.

| Parameter | Concept ID / revision | Type / unit | Default / required | Group | Summary |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| <code>vis</code> | <code>data.input.vis@r3</code> | <code>path (measurement_set)</code> | <code>""</code>; required | Input | One input MeasurementSet path. |
| <code>caltable</code> | <code>calibration.caltable@r2</code> | <code>path (calibration_table)</code> | <code>""</code>; required | Input | Calibration table the task writes. |
| <code>toffset</code> | <code>wvr.toffset@r1</code> | <code>float</code> | <code>0.0</code>; optional | Solve | Shift radiometer timestamps by this many seconds. |
| <code>segsource</code> | <code>wvr.segsource@r1</code> | <code>bool</code> | <code>true</code>; optional | Solve | Fit atmospheric coefficients separately for each contiguous source group. |
| <code>sourceflag</code> | <code>wvr.sourceflag@r1</code> | <code>array&lt;string&gt;</code> | <code>[]</code>; optional | Solve | Exclude these source names from the path solutions. |
| <code>tie</code> | <code>wvr.tie@r1</code> | <code>array&lt;string&gt;</code> | <code>[]</code>; optional | Solve | Comma-separated groups of source names or IDs that share a fit. |
| <code>nsol</code> | <code>wvr.nsol@r1</code> | <code>integer</code> | <code>1</code>; optional | Solve | Number of fits for unsegmented data. |
| <code>disperse</code> | <code>wvr.disperse@r1</code> | <code>bool</code> | <code>false</code>; optional | Solve | Apply the frequency-dependent wet-path dispersion correction. |
| <code>wvrflag</code> | <code>wvr.wvrflag@r1</code> | <code>array&lt;string&gt;</code> | <code>[]</code>; optional | Solve | Replace radiometer data for the specified antenna names or IDs. |
| <code>statfield</code> | <code>wvr.statfield@r1</code> | <code>string</code> | <code>""</code>; optional | Solve | Limit reported statistics to this field name or ID. |
| <code>statsource</code> | <code>wvr.statsource@r1</code> | <code>string</code> | <code>""</code>; optional | Solve | Limit reported statistics to this source name. |
| <code>smooth</code> | <code>wvr.smooth@r1</code> | <code>string</code> | <code>""</code>; optional | Solve | Mean smoothing interval for the output calibration table, such as 10s. |
| <code>scale</code> | <code>wvr.scale@r1</code> | <code>float</code> | <code>1.0</code>; optional | Solve | Multiply the wet path by this factor before converting it to phase. |
| <code>spw</code> | <code>ms.selection.spw@r7</code> | <code>array&lt;integer&gt;</code> | <code>[]</code>; optional | Solve | Write corrections for these spectral-window IDs. An empty list selects all. |
| <code>wvrspw</code> | <code>wvr.wvrspw@r1</code> | <code>array&lt;integer&gt;</code> | <code>[]</code>; optional | Solve | Read radiometer data from these four-channel spectral windows. |
| <code>reversespw</code> | <code>wvr.reversespw@r1</code> | <code>string</code> | <code>""</code>; optional | Solve | Reverse the phase sign on selected spectral windows. |
| <code>cont</code> | <code>wvr.cont@r1</code> | <code>bool</code> | <code>false</code>; optional | Solve | Fit the atmospheric continuum opacity in one fixed-interval solution. |
| <code>maxdistm</code> | <code>wvr.maxdistm@r1</code> | <code>float</code> | <code>500.0</code>; optional | Solve | Maximum distance in meters from an antenna to a replacement radiometer. |
| <code>minnumants</code> | <code>wvr.minnumants@r1</code> | <code>integer</code> | <code>2</code>; optional | Solve | Minimum number of eligible neighboring radiometers for replacement. |
| <code>mingoodfrac</code> | <code>wvr.mingoodfrac@r1</code> | <code>float</code> | <code>0.8</code>; optional | Solve | Minimum good fraction required to use an antenna’s measured temperatures. |
| <code>usefieldtab</code> | <code>wvr.usefieldtab@r1</code> | <code>bool</code> | <code>false</code>; optional | Solve | Use the FIELD direction in place of recorded POINTING elevations. |
| <code>refant</code> | <code>wvr.refant@r1</code> | <code>array&lt;string&gt;</code> | <code>[]</code>; optional | Solve | Preferred reference antenna names or IDs in order. |
| <code>offsetstable</code> | <code>wvr.offsetstable@r1</code> | <code>path (table)</code> | <code>""</code>; optional | Solve | Subtract temperatures from this matching radiometer offsets table. |
| <code>rseed</code> | <code>wvr.rseed@r1</code> | <code>integer</code> | <code>0</code>; optional | Solve | Seed the atmospheric fit. Zero selects CASA’s default seed. |

<a id="surface-polcal"></a>

## Polarization Calibration (<code>polcal</code>)

- Kind: `task`
- Contract version: `1`
- Category: Calibration
- Provider family: `calibration`
- Summary: Solve polarization leakage and cross-hand calibration.

| Parameter | Concept ID / revision | Type / unit | Default / required | Group | Summary |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| <code>vis</code> | <code>data.input.vis@r3</code> | <code>path (measurement_set)</code> | <code>""</code>; required | Input | One input MeasurementSet path. |
| <code>caltable</code> | <code>calibration.caltable@r2</code> | <code>path (calibration_table)</code> | <code>""</code>; required | Solve | Calibration table the task writes. |
| <code>field</code> | <code>ms.selection.field@r1</code> | <code>string</code> | <code>""</code>; optional | Selection | CASA field selector.<br><em>Surface:</em> This calibration provider currently accepts comma-separated FIELD_ID integers. |
| <code>spw</code> | <code>ms.selection.spw@r1</code> | <code>string</code> | <code>""</code>; optional | Selection | Comma-separated SPECTRAL_WINDOW_ID integers |
| <code>intent</code> | <code>ms.selection.intent@r3</code> | <code>string</code> | <code>""</code>; optional | Selection | Intent selector. |
| <code>selectdata</code> | <code>parameter.selectdata@r1</code> | <code>bool</code> | <code>true</code>; optional | Selection | Whether detailed data-selection controls are enabled.<br><em>Surface:</em> Enables the detailed selection controls for this calibration workflow. |
| <code>timerange</code> | <code>ms.selection.timerange@r1</code> | <code>string</code> | <code>""</code>; optional | Selection | Inclusive TIME range in MJD seconds |
| <code>uvrange</code> | <code>ms.selection.uvrange@r4</code> | <code>variant</code> | <code>""</code>; optional | Selection | Accepts a CASA variant and converts it to selector text during MeasurementSet selection. |
| <code>antenna</code> | <code>ms.selection.antenna@r1</code> | <code>string</code> | <code>""</code>; optional | Selection | Comma-separated antenna ids matched against ANTENNA1/ANTENNA2 |
| <code>scan</code> | <code>ms.selection.scan@r1</code> | <code>string</code> | <code>""</code>; optional | Selection | Comma-separated scan numbers |
| <code>observation</code> | <code>ms.selection.observation@r4</code> | <code>variant</code> | <code>""</code>; optional | Selection | Accepts selector text or an observation ID. Preserve the input type for HISTORY. |
| <code>msselect</code> | <code>ms.selection.msselect@r1</code> | <code>string</code> | <code>""</code>; optional | Selection | Raw TaQL WHERE expression to AND with the structured selectors |
| <code>solint</code> | <code>parameter.solint@r2</code> | <code>variant</code> | <code>"inf"</code>; optional | Solve | Gain solution interval specified as inf, int, a time such as 30s, or seconds as a number. |
| <code>combine</code> | <code>setsolve.combine@r1</code> | <code>string</code> | <code>"obs,scan"</code>; optional | Solve | Data axes a solution interval spans: 'scan', 'field', 'spw', 'obs', comma-separated; empty for none. |
| <code>preavg</code> | <code>setsolve.preavg@r1</code> | <code>float</code> | <code>300.0</code>; optional | Solve | Pre-averaging interval in seconds within a solution interval; -1 for none. |
| <code>refant</code> | <code>parameter.refant@r1</code> | <code>string</code> | <code>""</code>; optional | Solve | Reference antenna name or id for solve workflows |
| <code>minblperant</code> | <code>parameter.minblperant@r2</code> | <code>integer</code> | <code>4</code>; optional | Solve | Minimum unflagged baselines an antenna needs to be solved for |
| <code>minsnr</code> | <code>setsolve.minsnr@r1</code> | <code>float</code> | <code>3.0</code>; optional | Solve | Solutions below this SNR are flagged. |
| <code>poltype</code> | <code>polcal.poltype@r1</code> | <code>choice (20 values)</code> | <code>"D+QU"</code>; optional | Solve | Polarization calibration solution type. |
| <code>smodel</code> | <code>setsolve.smodel@r1</code> | <code>array&lt;float&gt;</code> | <code>[]</code>; optional | Solve | Point-source Stokes model [I, Q, U, V] in Jy; empty for the MODEL_DATA column. |
| <code>append</code> | <code>setsolve.append@r1</code> | <code>bool</code> | <code>false</code>; optional | Solve | Append the solutions to an existing calibration table. |
| <code>docallib</code> | <code>mstransform.docallib@r1</code> | <code>bool</code> | <code>false</code>; optional | Calibration | Apply calibration from callib on the fly. |
| <code>callib</code> | <code>parameter.callib@r1</code> | <code>path (calibration_table)</code> | <code>""</code>; optional | Input | CASA callibrary file describing the calibration-table chain |
| <code>gaintable</code> | <code>setapply.gaintable@r1</code> | <code>array&lt;path (calibration_table)&gt;</code> | <code>[]</code>; optional | Input | Calibration tables to apply. |
| <code>gainfield</code> | <code>setapply.gainfield@r1</code> | <code>array&lt;string&gt;</code> | <code>[]</code>; optional | Apply | Field selection of the solutions to apply from each gain table; empty for all. |
| <code>interp</code> | <code>setapply.interp@r1</code> | <code>array&lt;string&gt;</code> | <code>[]</code>; optional | Apply | Interpolation in time (and frequency) for each gain table; empty for 'linear'. |
| <code>spwmap</code> | <code>setapply.spwmap@r1</code> | <code>array&lt;array&lt;integer&gt;&gt;</code> | <code>[]</code>; optional | Apply | Spectral-window map for each gain table: the window whose solutions each data window takes. |

| <code>calmode</code> | <code>single_dish.sky.calmode@r1</code> | <code>choice (12 values)</code> | <code>"ps"</code>; optional | Sky | Single-dish sky calibration mode. |

## Single-dish spectral baselines (<code>sdbaseline</code>)

- Kind: `task`
- Contract version: `1`
- Category: Calibration
- Provider family: `calibration`
- Summary: Fit, subtract and apply spectral baselines to selected single-dish observations.
